## Current state (2026-11-01)

Lookup table of every refuted idea (AGENTS.md: check it before proposing anything). Each row: row number, date, idea, verdict as recorded + deciding number, pointer to the verbatim archive line. Row numbers are the citation keys and are unchanged. Truncated cells end in "…"; the archive line has the full text.

- **Defaults unchanged** by rows 1469-1484. Family rules tried 2026-10-29/30 (1469 U1-intron, 1470 dispersion, 1471 sketchfam, 1473 reprule-2, 1474 reprule-3, 1475 chain-gate) all FAIL; tools stay opt-in; most-reads representative stays default.
- **Opt-in / proposed, not adopted:** 1472 semi-guided families PASS both held-outs, not a default (auditor first); 1476 `--absorb-loci` PROPOSED as default after OR6737 PASS by equality, user decides; 1483 `--tie-ms` stays opt-in (FAIL B2/B3), next = HG002 truth with B2 on final placement.
- **Failed / not adopted:** 1479 FAMASM multi-copy assembly (HG002 multi precision 14.0 -> 8.5); 1478 IsoCon WORSE; 1480 protein reconciler NULL on de novo families.
- **Reports:** 1477 ms-vs-AS (port justified); 1482 SQANTI single-copy chr20 (ours leads); 1484 PSV VCF (bcftools 54.6% / 93.9% at MAPQ 60).
- **1481 O2 LEVERS:** arm U FAILED the 57-family fast panel (assigned 59 < 63), stays opt-in; arm C PARKED untested (candidate_copies pass A cost).
- Catalog provenance warning still applies: /494 and /1415 figures are the superseded 2026-07-17 catalog (current default 627 families / 2,019 copies).

⚠ CATALOG PROVENANCE. Figures quoted per /494 (families) or /1415 (copies) describe the superseded 2026-07-17 catalog, which no invocation of the current binary reproduces — it was built with `refine` on, a default…
Last updated 2026-10-07 (header; rows are appended continuously).

### The three lessons that generalise past these nine (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:92)

⚠⚠ MEASURE AN EDGE RULE ON THE WHOLE EDGE SET. The 164-pair frozen arms are 6% of the 2,727 shipped edges and are where the FPs were.

### And one self-inflicted error worth not repeating (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:105)

The junction rule's TP cost was first computed as zero, on the ground that all 9 losses were single-exon genes.

# §3 THE FALSE-OMISSION MEASUREMENT (2026-08-14) (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:162)

Answer to "does the 53.8% of absorbed orphans landing on a paralog the family lacks mean O1 missed 53.8% of its members?" — No.
Artifacts: `/home/juanfra/winloci_scratch/o1_falseomission/` — `RESULTS.md`, `per_family_er_ALL.tsv` (728 tests), `SUMMARY.txt`, `nonsister_calls.json`, `testis_depth.json`.

### 3.1 The sanity control saved the result (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:171)

The brief's assumed substrate was wrong.

|---|---|---|---|

Run as literally specified (genomic intervals + read-span fallback) the experiment returns 43% false omission — pure artefact: destination spans are read-*start* spans (one is 135 bp) and `denom="min"` is structurally…

### 3.2 Population (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:188)

162 panel families (design, frozen before masking) · absorbed 104/162 = 0.6420 [0.5656, 0.7117] · non-sister 56/104 = 0.5385 [0.4430, 0.6312].

### 3.3 ARM 1 — is the destination even a node? (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:193)

Non-sister destinations that are nodes in O1's catalog at all: 2/56 = 0.0357 [0.0098, 0.1212].

### 3.4 The tissue excuse is REFUTED (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:199)

Catalog substrate is `GGO_ds.bam` (OR6737 testis, written 26 min before the catalog); excision reads are fibroblast.

### 3.7 Rate, both denominators (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:227)

PRIMARY denominator is the 162 panel families (frozen before masking, part of the design); 104 and 56 are experiment OUTCOMES and therefore secondary (see T1).
| key | date | idea | verdict / deciding number (pointer) |
|---|---|---|---|
| **(a) ARM 3, unbiased** | | 9/162 = 0.0556 [0.0295, 0.1022] | 0.1607 [0.0869, 0.2781] (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:234) |
| (a) ARM 2, upper bound | | 0.2407 [0.1814, 0.3121] | 0.6964 [0.5666, 0.8010] (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:235) |
| **(b) no edge** | | 47/56 = 0.8393 [0.7219, 0.9131] | 47/56 = 0.8393 [0.7219, 0.9131] (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:236) |
Quote 9/162 = 5.6% [3.0, 10.2] as O1's false-omission rate, with 24.1% [18.1, 31.2] as the pro-edge upper bound.
Blunt version: *"53.8% of absorbed families sent orphans to a locus the family lacks"* is true about where reads go.

### 3.8 Caveats that could move it (full: archive/2026-11/NEGATIVE_RESULTS_REGISTER.md:245)

ARM 3's control ceiling is 0.8558, and 29/56 destinations built no testis rep (counted as no-edge) ⟹ 9/56 is a floor within ARM 3; the conditioned 9/27 is deliberately NOT quoted (T1).
